--- function getStationNamingTypeInfo lines 3346-3380 ---
003346:     function getStationNamingTypeInfo(buildingTypeId, displayName) {
003347:         const name = cleanText(displayName).toUpperCase();
003348: 
003349:         // Airport fire stations share MissionChief's normal fire-station type IDs.
003350:         // Use an explicit existing name marker only for this special case.
003351:         if ((buildingTypeId === 0 || buildingTypeId === 18)
003352:             && (/\bAIRPORT\b|\bAIRFIELD\b/.test(name) || /-AF\d*$/.test(name))) {
003353:             return { stationType: 'AIRFIELD', suffix: '-AF', label: 'Airfield station' };
003354:         }
003355: 
003356:         const mapped = STATION_BUILDING_TYPE_INFO[buildingTypeId];
003357:         if (mapped) return mapped;
003358: 
003359:         const detectedType = detectStationType(displayName);
003360:         const fallbackByType = {
003361:             FIRE: { stationType: 'FIRE', suffix: '-FS', label: 'Fire station' },
003362:             AMBULANCE: { stationType: 'AMBULANCE', suffix: '-AS', label: 'Ambulance station' },
003363:             POLICE: { stationType: 'POLICE', suffix: '-PS', label: 'Police station' },
003364:             RNLI: { stationType: 'RNLI', suffix: '-RNLI', label: 'RNLI / lifeboat station' },
003365:             COASTGUARD: { stationType: 'COASTGUARD', suffix: '-CG', label: 'Coastguard station' },
003366:             SAR: { stationType: 'SAR', suffix: '-SAR', label: 'Search and rescue station' },
003367:             AIRFIELD: { stationType: 'AIRFIELD', suffix: '-AF', label: 'Airfield station' },
003368:             EOD: { stationType: 'EOD', suffix: '-EOD', label: 'EOD / bomb disposal station' },
003369:             RECOVERY: { stationType: 'RECOVERY', suffix: '-REC', label: 'Recovery station' }
003370:         };
003371: 
003372:         if (detectedType === 'AIR') {
003373:             return /POLICE/i.test(name)
003374:                 ? { stationType: 'AIR', suffix: '-PH', label: 'Police helicopter station' }
003375:                 : { stationType: 'AIR', suffix: '-AA', label: 'Air ambulance station' };
003376:         }
003377: 
003378:         return fallbackByType[detectedType]
003379:             || { stationType: 'OTHER', suffix: '', label: `Unsupported building type ${buildingTypeId ?? 'unknown'}` };
003380:     }

--- function getPersonnelStationScopeLabel lines 4308-4317 ---
004308:     function getPersonnelStationScopeLabel() {
004309:         const service = getPersonnelServiceDefinition();
004310:         const stationTypes = getPersonnelProfileStationTypes();
004311: 
004312:         if (service === PERSONNEL_SERVICE_DEFINITIONS.medical) return 'ambulance stations';
004313:         if (stationTypes.length === 1 && stationTypes[0] === 'AIR') return 'police aviation stations';
004314:         if (stationTypes.length === 1 && stationTypes[0] === 'EOD') return 'EOD / bomb disposal stations';
004315:         if (service === PERSONNEL_SERVICE_DEFINITIONS.police) return 'relevant Police stations';
004316:         return `relevant ${service.label} stations`;
004317:     }

--- function getPersonnelAmbulanceQueue lines 7338-7407 ---
007338:     function getPersonnelAmbulanceQueue(doc) {
007339:         const seen = new Set();
007340:         const queue = [];
007341:         const rows = [...doc.querySelectorAll('#vehicle_table tr')];
007342:         let ambulanceRows = 0;
007343: 
007344:         for (const row of rows) {
007345:             const image = row.querySelector('img[vehicle_type_id]');
007346:             const vehicleTypeId = image?.getAttribute('vehicle_type_id') || '';
007347:             if (vehicleTypeId !== PERSONNEL_TARGET_VEHICLE_TYPE_ID) continue;
007348: 
007349:             ambulanceRows++;
007350: 
007351:             const vehicleLinks = [...row.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/vehicles/"]')];
007352:             const plainVehicleLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
007353:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
007354:                 return /^\/vehicles\/\d+\/?(?:[?#].*)?$/.test(href);
007355:             });
007356:             const editLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
007357:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
007358:                 return /\/vehicles\/\d+\/edit(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(href);
007359:             });
007360:             const directAssignmentLink = vehicleLinks.find(link => {
007361:                 const href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
007362:                 return /\/vehicles\/\d+\/zuweisung(?:[/?#]|$)/.test(href);
007363:             });
007364: 
007365:             // Prefer the normal vehicle-name link because it is tied directly to the
007366:             // displayed row. Edit and personnel links are safe fallbacks.
007367:             const idCandidates = [plainVehicleLink, editLink, directAssignmentLink]
007368:                 .map(link => getVehicleIdFromHref(link?.getAttribute('href') || ''))
007369:                 .filter(Boolean);
007370:             const vehicleId = idCandidates[0] || '';
007371: 
007372:             if (!vehicleId || seen.has(vehicleId)) continue;
007373:             seen.add(vehicleId);
007374: 
007375:             const distinctIds = [...new Set(idCandidates)];
007376:             if (distinctIds.length > 1) {
007377:                 personnelDebug(`Ambulance row contains conflicting vehicle IDs (${distinctIds.join(', ')}). Using ${vehicleId}.`);
007378:             }
007379: 
007380:             const assignmentHref = directAssignmentLink?.getAttribute('href') || `/vehicles/${vehicleId}/zuweisung`;
007381:             const nameSource = plainVehicleLink
007382:                 || vehicleLinks.find(link => cleanText(link.textContent || ''))
007383:                 || null;
007384:             const name = cleanText(nameSource?.textContent || row.textContent || '') || `Ambulance ${vehicleId}`;
007385:             const occupancy = parsePersonnelVehicleOccupancy(row);
007386: 
007387:             queue.push({
007388:                 vehicleId,
007389:                 name,
007390:                 assignmentHref,
007391:                 assignedCount: occupancy.assignedCount,
007392:                 maxCount: occupancy.maxCount
007393:             });
007394:         }
007395: 
007396:         queue.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, {
007397:             numeric: true,
007398:             sensitivity: 'base'
007399:         }));
007400: 
007401:         personnelDebug(`Vehicle table scan: ${rows.length} row(s), ${ambulanceRows} Ambulance row(s), ${queue.length} usable assignment route(s).`);
007402:         queue.forEach(vehicle => {
007403:             personnelDebug(`Ambulance detected: ${vehicle.name} | ID=${vehicle.vehicleId} | table occupancy=${vehicle.assignedCount ?? '?'} / ${vehicle.maxCount ?? '?'}`);
007404:         });
007405: 
007406:         return queue;
007407:     }

012234:     function getVehicleMatchCandidates(originalName, mappedName) {
012235:         const raw = String(originalName || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();

017731:     function triggerAmbulanceClick(number) {

017734:         }

017750:                     domSelected,
017751:                     trackedSelected

017754:             );

--- function synchroniseMissionInstanceState lines 18255-18332 ---
018255:     function synchroniseMissionInstanceState(reason = '') {
018256:         const nextMissionKey =
018257:             getLocalMissionInstanceKey();
018258: 
018259:         if (!mfCurrentMissionInstanceKey) {
018260:             mfCurrentMissionInstanceKey = nextMissionKey;
018261:             return false;
018262:         }
018263: 
018264:         if (
018265:             !nextMissionKey ||
018266:             nextMissionKey === mfCurrentMissionInstanceKey
018267:         ) {
018268:             return false;
018269:         }
018270: 
018271:         const currentKeyIsStrong =
018272:             /^mission:\d+$/.test(
018273:                 mfCurrentMissionInstanceKey
018274:             );
018275: 
018276:         const nextKeyIsStrong =
018277:             /^mission:\d+$/.test(
018278:                 nextMissionKey
018279:             );
018280: 
018281:         // The mission ID can appear a fraction later than the initial DOM.
018282:         // Promote a provisional document key to the real mission ID without
018283:         // treating that as a second mission and clearing valid selections.
018284:         if (!currentKeyIsStrong && nextKeyIsStrong) {
018285:             mfCurrentMissionInstanceKey = nextMissionKey;
018286:             invalidateMissionContextCaches();
018287:             invalidatePatientCountCache();
018288:             return false;
018289:         }
018290: 
018291:         // Do not demote a known mission ID during a brief partial render.
018292:         if (currentKeyIsStrong && !nextKeyIsStrong) {
018293:             return false;
018294:         }
018295: 
018296:         mfCurrentMissionInstanceKey = nextMissionKey;
018297: 
018298:         invalidateMissionContextCaches();
018299:         invalidatePatientCountCache();
018300:         invalidateVehicleCheckboxCache();
018301:         clearSelectionGuards();
018302:         clearAutoSelectionMissionGuard(
018303:             `mission changed: ${reason || 'shared reader'}`
018304:         );
018305: 
018306:         mfPatientSelectionLedgerCache = null;
018307: 
018308:         try {
018309:             sessionStorage.removeItem(
018310:                 MF_PATIENT_SELECTION_LEDGER_KEY
018311:             );
018312:         } catch (_error) {}
018313: 
018314:         mfStaffingBlockActive = false;
018315:         mfStaffingBlockText = '';
018316:         mfStaffingBlockMissionKey = '';
018317:         mfMissionUpdateFirstPassMissionKey = '';
018318:         mfMissionUpdateFirstPassRequired = true;
018319:         mfMissionUpdateFirstPassCompletedAt = 0;
018320: 
018321:         vehicleLoadState = {
018322:             rows: [],
018323:             patients: 0,
018324:             ambulances: 0,
018325:             ambulanceOfficer: false,
018326:             ready: false
018327:         };
018328: 
018329:         renderVehicleLoadList();
018330: 
018331:         return true;
018332:     }

--- function shouldSkipLiveRequirement lines 20612-20646 ---
020612:     function shouldSkipLiveRequirement(name, value) {
020613:         const cleanedName = cleanRequirementName(name);
020614:         const text = `${cleanedName} ${value}`.toLowerCase();
020615:         const mappedName = resolveUnitName(cleanedName);
020616: 
020617:         if (!cleanedName) return true;
020618:         if (shouldIgnoreRequiredMinimumRequirement(name) ||
020619:             shouldIgnoreRequiredMinimumRequirement(cleanedName)) return true;
020620:         if (!/^\d+$/.test(String(value || '').trim())) return true;
020621: 
020622:         // V9.0.4: Ambulances are selected from the patient badge first, not from the live mission help table.
020623:         // Mission Update still handles later patient-specific ambulance requests.
020624:         if (normaliseVehicleText(mappedName) === normaliseVehicleText('Ambulance x 01')) return true;
020625: 
020626:         // V9.2.7: these are mission stats/preconditions, not vehicles to send.
020627:         // Example: Domestic flooding (Large) has "Min. Pump Capacity = 1000".
020628:         // That is litres/min pump capacity, NOT Pump x1000.
020629:         if (/^min\.?\s*pump\s*capacity$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020630:         if (/^minimum\s*pump\s*capacity$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020631:         if (/^pump\s*capacity$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020632:         if (/^water\s*to\s*pump$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020633:         if (/water\s*damage\s*pump\s*extension/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020634:         if (/\bextension\b/i.test(cleanedName) && !/vehicle|unit|officer|car|engine|pump$/i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020635: 
020636:         if (/only required/.test(text)) return true;
020637:         if (/^probability of /i.test(cleanedName)) return true;
020638:         if (/\bprobability\b/.test(text)) return true;
020639:         if (/personnel/.test(text)) return true;
020640:         if (/patients?/.test(text)) return true;
020641:         if (/prisoners?/.test(text)) return true;
020642:         if (/transport request/.test(text)) return true;
020643:         if (/chance/.test(text)) return true;
020644: 
020645:         return false;
020646:     }

--- function applyLatePatientCount lines 26001-26053 ---
026001:     function applyLatePatientCount(patientCount, sourceLabel) {
026002:         const required = Math.max(0, parseInt(patientCount, 10) || 0);
026003: 
026004:         if (required <= 0) return false;
026005: 
026006:         const alreadySelected = countSelectedMatchingVehicles('Ambulance', 'Ambulance x 01');
026007:         const stillNeeded = Math.max(required - alreadySelected, 0);
026008: 
026009:         vehicleLoadState.patients = required;
026010: 
026011:         addOrUpdateVehicleRow(
026012:             'Ambulance',
026013:             'Ambulance x 01',
026014:             required,
026015:             stillNeeded > 0 ? 'pending' : 'assigned',
026016:             Math.min(alreadySelected, required)
026017:         );
026018: 
026019:         if (stillNeeded > 0) {
026020:             if (mfDebugEnabled) {
026021:                 debugLog('LATE PATIENT', `${sourceLabel || 'retry'} found ${required} patient(s); selecting ${stillNeeded} remaining ambulance(s).`);
026022:             }
026023: 
026024:             selectVehicleUnits('Ambulance', 'Ambulance x 01', stillNeeded, 'PATIENT-LATE');
026025:         }
026026: 
026027:         const selectedNow = Math.min(
026028:             countSelectedMatchingVehicles('Ambulance', 'Ambulance x 01'),
026029:             required
026030:         );
026031: 
026032:         vehicleLoadState.ambulances = selectedNow;
026033: 
026034:         addOrUpdateVehicleRow(
026035:             'Ambulance',
026036:             'Ambulance x 01',
026037:             required,
026038:             selectedNow >= required ? 'assigned' : 'missing',
026039:             selectedNow
026040:         );
026041: 
026042:         renderVehicleLoadList();
026043: 
026044:         if (selectedNow >= required) {
026045:             updateStatusBox(`Late patient data loaded: ${required} ambulance(s) selected.`);
026046:             changeDispatchBoxColor(true);
026047:             return true;
026048:         }
026049: 
026050:         updateStatusBox(`Late patient data loaded, but only ${selectedNow}/${required} ambulance(s) were selected.`);
026051:         changeDispatchBoxColor(false);
026052:         return false;
026053:     }

--- function handleCombinedLogic lines 27155-27439 ---
027155:     async function handleCombinedLogic(options = {}) {
027156:         if (!isCurrentMissionExecutionOwner('Unit Finder')) {
027157:             return false;
027158:         }
027159: 
027160:         synchroniseMissionInstanceState('Unit Finder start');
027161: 
027162:         const missionKeyAtStart =
027163:             getLocalMissionInstanceKey();
027164: 
027165:         const vehicleListAlreadyLoaded =
027166:             !!options.vehicleListAlreadyLoaded;
027167: 
027168:         const suppliedAttachmentPromise =
027169:             options.attachmentRowsPromise &&
027170:             typeof options.attachmentRowsPromise.then === 'function'
027171:                 ? options.attachmentRowsPromise
027172:                 : null;
027173: 
027174:         resetVehicleLoadState();
027175:         resetStaffingBlockForCurrentMission(
027176:             'Unit Finder start'
027177:         );
027178: 
027179:         if (
027180:             detectAndLatchStaffingBlock(
027181:                 'Unit Finder precheck'
027182:             )
027183:         ) {
027184:             return;
027185:         }
027186: 
027187:         if (mfDebugEnabled) {
027188:             debugLog('START', `Unit Finder | Mission: ${getCurrentMissionName()}`, true);
027189:         }
027190:         changeDispatchBoxColor(false);
027191:         updateStatusBox('Started V9 live Unit Finder...');
027192: 
027193:         if (!vehicleListAlreadyLoaded) {
027194:             const vehicleLoadResult =
027195:                 await ensureVehicleListLoaded({
027196:                     requireNonZero: true
027197:                 });
027198: 
027199:             if (!vehicleLoadResult.ready) {
027200:                 vehicleLoadState.ready = false;
027201:                 changeDispatchBoxColor(false);
027202:                 updateStatusBox(
027203:                     'Unit Finder stopped: the complete vehicle list did not become stable.'
027204:                 );
027205:                 return false;
027206:             }
027207:         }
027208: 
027209:         if (
027210:             missionKeyAtStart !==
027211:             getLocalMissionInstanceKey()
027212:         ) {
027213:             updateStatusBox(
027214:                 'Mission changed while Unit Finder was loading. Previous mission cancelled.'
027215:             );
027216:             return;
027217:         }
027218: 
027219:         // Keep the initial and update workflows separate:
027220:         //   Unit Finder    -> mission-help attachment
027221:         //                     Vehicle and Personnel Requirements
027222:         //   Mission Update -> Live Mission Requirements panel
027223:         //
027224:         // The live panel can exist during the initial mission. It must not
027225:         // prevent Unit Finder from fetching the mission-help attachment.
027226: 
027227:         if (
027228:             mfDebugEnabled
027229:         ) {
027230:             debugLog(
027231:                 'UNIT FINDER SOURCE',
027232:                 'Current visible Missing Vehicles/Personnel is checked first. The full mission-help attachment is used only when no explicit current shortage exists.'
027233:             );
027234:         }
027235: 
027236:         let patientCount = handlePatientSelector();
027237: 
027238:         let currentUpdateRows =
027239:             readMissionUpdateRows({
027240:                 silent: true
027241:             });
027242: 
027243:         let explicitMissingRows =
027244:             getExplicitCurrentMissingRequirementRows(
027245:                 currentUpdateRows
027246:             );
027247: 
027248:         let useExplicitMissingRequirements =
027249:             explicitMissingRows.length > 0;
027250: 
027251:         const attachmentRows =
027252:             useExplicitMissingRequirements
027253:                 ? []
027254:                 : suppliedAttachmentPromise
027255:                     ? await suppliedAttachmentPromise
027256:                     : await readLiveMissionRequirements();
027257: 
027258:         // A current Missing Vehicles/Personnel alert can render while the
027259:         // mission-help request is in flight. Re-read the current mission once
027260:         // before acting so a late authoritative shortage still suppresses the
027261:         // full static requirement set.
027262:         if (!useExplicitMissingRequirements) {
027263:             const refreshedUpdateRows =
027264:                 readMissionUpdateRows();
027265: 
027266:             const refreshedExplicitMissingRows =
027267:                 getExplicitCurrentMissingRequirementRows(
027268:                     refreshedUpdateRows
027269:                 );
027270: 
027271:             if (refreshedExplicitMissingRows.length > 0) {
027272:                 currentUpdateRows =
027273:                     refreshedUpdateRows;
027274: 
027275:                 explicitMissingRows =
027276:                     refreshedExplicitMissingRows;
027277: 
027278:                 useExplicitMissingRequirements =
027279:                     true;
027280:             }
027281:         }
027282: 
027283:         if (
027284:             missionKeyAtStart !==
027285:             getLocalMissionInstanceKey()
027286:         ) {
027287:             updateStatusBox(
027288:                 'Mission changed before Unit Finder selection. Previous mission requirements ignored.'
027289:             );
027290:             return;
027291:         }
027292: 
027293:         const requirementReadFailure =
027294:             useExplicitMissingRequirements
027295:                 ? null
027296:                 : getMissionRequirementReadFailure(
027297:                     missionKeyAtStart
027298:                 );
027299: 
027300:         if (requirementReadFailure) {
027301:             changeDispatchBoxColor(false);
027302:             updateStatusBox(
027303:                 `Unit Finder stopped: ${requirementReadFailure.error || 'authoritative mission requirements were unavailable.'}`
027304:             );
027305:             renderVehicleLoadList();
027306:             return false;
027307:         }
027308: 
027309:         // Patient cards can finish rendering while the attachment is being
027310:         // fetched. Refresh the normal patient count once, then process the
027311:         // explicit current-patient "We need:" rows before the mission rows.
027312:         // Running the mission rows last ensures a missing mission unit cannot
027313:         // be hidden by a successful patient-only pass.
027314:         patientCount = Math.max(
027315:             patientCount,
027316:             handlePatientSelector()
027317:         );
027318: 
027319:         const patientRequirementResult =
027320:             handleUnitFinderPatientRequirements();
027321: 
027322:         const preservePatientFailure = result => {
027323:             if (
027324:                 patientRequirementResult.found &&
027325:                 !patientRequirementResult.satisfied
027326:             ) {
027327:                 changeDispatchBoxColor(false);
027328:                 return false;
027329:             }
027330: 
027331:             return result;
027332:         };
027333: 
027334:         if (useExplicitMissingRequirements) {
027335:             updateStatusBox(
027336:                 `Current missing requirements found: ${explicitMissingRows.length} row(s). Full mission requirements were not reloaded.`
027337:             );
027338: 
027339:             if (mfDebugEnabled) {
027340:                 debugLog(
027341:                     'UNIT FINDER MISSING AUTHORITY',
027342:                     explicitMissingRows
027343:                         .map(row => `${row.unitName} x${row.stillNeeded}`)
027344:                         .join(' | ')
027345:                 );
027346:             }
027347: 
027348:             // readMissionUpdateRows has already removed unrelated full mission
027349:             // totals while retaining current patient shortages. Process that
027350:             // authoritative set rather than dropping patient rows by passing
027351:             // only the explicit vehicle/personnel subset.
027352:             const missionRequirementsSatisfied =
027353:                 await processRequirementRows(
027354:                     currentUpdateRows,
027355:                     'CURRENT MISSING REQUIREMENTS'
027356:                 );
027357: 
027358:             return preservePatientFailure(
027359:                 missionRequirementsSatisfied
027360:             );
027361:         }
027362: 
027363:         if (attachmentRows.length > 0) {
027364:             const missionRequirementsSatisfied =
027365:                 await processRequirementRows(
027366:                     attachmentRows,
027367:                     'MISSION HELP ATTACHMENT'
027368:                 );
027369: 
027370:             return preservePatientFailure(
027371:                 missionRequirementsSatisfied
027372:             );
027373:         }
027374: 
027375:         const visibleFallbackRows = parseVisibleMissionRequirementRows();
027376: 
027377:         if (visibleFallbackRows.length > 0) {
027378:             updateStatusBox(`Visible fallback requirements found: ${visibleFallbackRows.length} row(s).`);
027379: 
027380:             if (mfDebugEnabled) {
027381:                 debugLog(
027382:                     'UNIT FINDER ATTACHMENT FALLBACK',
027383:                     'Mission-help attachment returned 0 rows, using visible mission requirements instead.'
027384:                 );
027385:             }
027386: 
027387:             const missionRequirementsSatisfied =
027388:                 await processRequirementRows(
027389:                     visibleFallbackRows,
027390:                     'VISIBLE FALLBACK'
027391:                 );
027392: 
027393:             return preservePatientFailure(
027394:                 missionRequirementsSatisfied
027395:             );
027396:         }
027397: 
027398:         const vehicleList = findLegacyVehicleRequirementList();
027399: 
027400:         if (vehicleList) {
027401:             updateStatusBox('Fallback vehicle list found. Processing...');
027402: 
027403:             const missionRequirementsSatisfied =
027404:                 await processVehicles(vehicleList);
027405: 
027406:             return preservePatientFailure(
027407:                 missionRequirementsSatisfied
027408:             );
027409:         }
027410: 
027411:         if (
027412:             patientCount > 0 ||
027413:             patientRequirementResult.found
027414:         ) {
027415:             if (patientRequirementResult.satisfied) {
027416:                 updateStatusBox(
027417:                     'No mission vehicle rows found. Current patient requirements processed.'
027418:                 );
027419:                 changeDispatchBoxColor(true);
027420:                 return true;
027421:             }
027422: 
027423:             updateStatusBox(
027424:                 'Current patient requirements are still missing units.'
027425:             );
027426:             changeDispatchBoxColor(false);
027427:             return false;
027428:         }
027429: 
027430:         // MissionChief sometimes opens the mission before its patient cards have
027431:         // finished rendering. Do not immediately decide that the mission has no units.
027432:         const recovered = await recoverLateMissionRequirements(patientCount);
027433: 
027434:         if (recovered) return;
027435: 
027436:         updateStatusBox('No live requirements or fallback vehicle list found after retrying.');
027437:         changeDispatchBoxColor(false);
027438:         renderVehicleLoadList();
027439:     }

--- function getMissionUpdatePatientRequirementRule lines 28870-28929 ---
028870:     function getMissionUpdatePatientRequirementRule(
028871:         unitName
028872:     ) {
028873:         const cleaned =
028874:             String(
028875:                 unitName ||
028876:                 ''
028877:             )
028878:                 .replace(
028879:                     /\s+/g,
028880:                     ' '
028881:                 )
028882:                 .trim();
028883: 
028884:         if (
028885:             /^(?:Required\s+)?Ambulance\s+Officer(?:s)?$/i
028886:                 .test(cleaned)
028887:         ) {
028888:             return {
028889:                 type: 'ambulance-officer',
028890:                 unitName: 'Ambulance Officer',
028891:                 maximum: 1
028892:             };
028893:         }
028894: 
028895:         if (
028896:             /^(?:Required\s+)?Mass\s+Casualty\s+Equipment(?:\s+Unit(?:s)?)?$/i
028897:                 .test(cleaned)
028898:         ) {
028899:             return {
028900:                 type: 'mass-casualty-equipment',
028901:                 unitName: 'Mass Casualty Equipment',
028902:                 maximum: 1
028903:             };
028904:         }
028905: 
028906:         if (
028907:             /^(?:Required\s+)?Ambulance(?:s)?(?:\s+x\s*0?1)?$/i
028908:                 .test(cleaned)
028909:         ) {
028910:             return {
028911:                 type: 'ambulance',
028912:                 unitName: 'Ambulance x 01',
028913:                 maximum: null
028914:             };
028915:         }
028916: 
028917:         if (
028918:             /^(?:Required\s+)?Critical\s+Care(?:\s+Team(?:s)?)?$/i
028919:                 .test(cleaned)
028920:         ) {
028921:             return {
028922:                 type: 'critical-care',
028923:                 unitName: 'Critical Care',
028924:                 maximum: null
028925:             };
028926:         }
028927: 
028928:         return null;
028929:     }

--- function isCurrentMissionPatientAlert lines 29362-29430 ---
029362:     function isCurrentMissionPatientAlert(
029363:         alertElement,
029364:         roots,
029365:         hasLiveRequirementsPanel,
029366:         visiblePatientBadgeCount
029367:     ) {
029368:         if (
029369:             !alertElement ||
029370:             !isMissionElementVisible(
029371:                 alertElement
029372:             )
029373:         ) {
029374:             return false;
029375:         }
029376: 
029377:         if (
029378:             alertElement.closest?.(
029379:                 '#mission-finder-wrapper'
029380:             )
029381:         ) {
029382:             return false;
029383:         }
029384: 
029385:         const text =
029386:             normaliseMissionAlertText(
029387:                 alertElement.textContent ||
029388:                 alertElement.innerText ||
029389:                 ''
029390:             );
029391: 
029392:         if (
029393:             !/\bWe\s+need\s*:/i.test(
029394:                 text
029395:             )
029396:         ) {
029397:             return false;
029398:         }
029399: 
029400:         // Keep the legacy behaviour when the dedicated live panel does not
029401:         // exist. The stricter scope guard is specifically for the live-panel
029402:         // route where stale previous-patient alerts were being picked up.
029403:         if (!hasLiveRequirementsPanel) {
029404:             return true;
029405:         }
029406: 
029407:         if (
029408:             getLinkedVisiblePatientContainer(
029409:                 alertElement,
029410:                 roots
029411:             )
029412:         ) {
029413:             return true;
029414:         }
029415: 
029416:         // Some MissionChief layouts temporarily place the alert outside the
029417:         // patient card. Accept that fallback only when the current mission
029418:         // container also has a visible positive Patients badge. A leftover
029419:         // patient alert from a previous mission cannot then add an ambulance
029420:         // to a Police-only Mission Update.
029421:         return (
029422:             Math.max(
029423:                 0,
029424:                 parseInt(
029425:                     visiblePatientBadgeCount,
029426:                     10
029427:                 ) || 0
029428:             ) > 0
029429:         );
029430:     }

032085:     async function autoHandleMissionUpdateAfterDispatch() {
032086:         const rows = readMissionUpdateRows();
032087: 
032088:         if (rows.length === 0) {
032089:             updateStatusBox("No mission update found after dispatch.");
032090:             return;
032091:         }
032092: 
032093:         updateStatusBox("Mission update found. Assigning new units...");
032094: 
032095:         await preparePoliceVehicleSafetyForRows(
032096:             rows,
032097:             'AUTO POST-DISPATCH UPDATE'
032098:         );
032099: 
032100:         const assigned = handleMissionUpdateUnits(false, rows);
032101: 
032102:         if (assigned) {
032103:             setTimeout(() => {
032104:                 updateStatusBox("Mission update assigned. Ready to dispatch again.");
032105:                 changeDispatchBoxColor(true);
032106:             }, 1000);
032107:         }

037728:     async function retryAutoSelectionWhenZero() {

037842:     async function runAutoModeLoop() {
037843:         if (isManualAutoStopActive()) return;
037844:         if (autoModeLoopActive) return;
037845: 
037846:         if (!isCurrentMissionExecutionOwner('Auto Mode start')) {
037847:             removeMissionFinderPanelForClosedMission(
037848:                 'inactive previous mission instance'
037849:             );
037850:             return;
037851:         }
037852: 
037853:         autoModeLoopActive = true;
037854: 
037855:         while (autoModeRunning && !isManualAutoStopActive()) {
037856:             if (!isCurrentMissionExecutionOwner('Auto Mode cycle')) {
037857:                 removeMissionFinderPanelForClosedMission(
037858:                     'another mission became active'
037859:                 );
037860:                 break;
037861:             }
037862:

037864:                 if (getMainMissionOpenLockRemaining() <= 0) {
037865:                     sessionStorage.removeItem(MF_QUEUE_OPENING_MISSION_FLAG);
037866:                     mfSilentQueueOpening = false;
037867:                     releaseMainMissionOpenLock('opening flag expired');
037868:

037870:                         debugLog('QUEUE OPEN GUARD', 'Expired queue-opening flag cleared.');
037871:                     }
037872:                 } else {
037873:                     updateStatusBox('Queue restart is opening the next mission...');
037874:                     await wait(350);
037875:                     continue;
037876:                 }
037877:             }
037878: 
037879:             if (sessionStorage.getItem(MF_FINAL_QUEUE_DISPATCH_FLAG) === 'true') {
037880:                 const restarted = await waitForQueueRestartAndOpenMission('same-screen-after-final-dispatch');
037881: 
037882:                 if (restarted) {
037883:                     continue;
037884:                 }
037885:
